and
V_{\mathcal{B}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}(V_{\beta}:\beta\in\mathcal{B})
.

Appendix C
Proof of Proposition
5
: Closeness of the states

We mimic the steps in
[
24
]
in this proof.
For any
\ket{h}\in\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ketbra{h})-\ketbra{h}}_{1}\leq 2\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(\bra{h})-\bra{h}\right)\left(\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>-<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),h>-<h,\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>+<h,h>

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle 2-\frac{2}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2-2e^{-2\eta^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\eta^{2}

where (a) follows from (i)
\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}
being an isometry and (ii)
<\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h),h>=<h,\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n},\eta}(h)>=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}}
, (b) follows from
\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\eta^{2}}}>e^{-2\eta^{2}}
, and (c) follows from
e^{-x}\geq 1-x
, for all
x\geq 0
.
Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ketbra{h})-\ketbra{h}}_{1}\leq 2\norm{\CalT_{d_1^n,d_2^n,\eta}(\ket{h})-\ket{h}}_{2}\leq 4\eta.

Appendix D
Proof of Proposition
6

We first establish a key property of the decoding POVM elements
G^{\mathcal{J}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
, for
\mathcal{J}\in\{0,1,2\}
, that will be used throughout the proof.
For
\mathcal{J}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{G^{1}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\pi^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}

\displaystyle\sum_{(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\norm{\pi_{u^n}^{Y_1} \rho^{Y_1}_{v_1^n,u^n} \pi_{u^n}^{Y_1}-\rho^{Y_1}_{v_1^n,u^n}}_{1}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\geq}

\displaystyle 1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon},

where (a) follows from the definition of
G^{1}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
in (
9
), (b) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (c) follows from the trace inequality
\tr\left(\Delta\rho\right)\geq\tr\left(\Delta\sigma\right)-\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, for
0\leq\Delta,\rho,\sigma\leq I
, and (d) follows from (i) the Gentle Operator Lemma
[
25
]
and from (ii) the typical projector property

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\mbox{ if }(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U}).

The same argument applies for
\mathcal{J}\in\{0,2\}
.

Now, we evaluate the expectation of
T_{1.2.1}
with respect to
\left(D_{1}^{n}(b_{1},m_{1}),D_{2}^{n}(a)\right)
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}-\tr\left\{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}+\beta^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\beta^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d^{n}_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\beta^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+\norm{\theta_{v_1^n,d^n_1,u^n,d^n_2}- \left(\rho^{Y_1}_{v_1^n,u^n}\otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{6}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\left[\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{1}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right)+\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{2}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right)+\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{0}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{=}

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{6}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\left[1-\tr\left\{G^{1}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right)+1-\tr\left\{G^{2}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right)+1-\tr\left\{G^{0}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 16\eta+\frac{72}{\eta^{2}}(\epsilon+2\sqrt{\epsilon}),

where (a) follows from (i) the definition of
\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}
in (
11
), (ii) the cyclicity of the trace and (iii) the property of projectors, (b) follows from applying the non-commutative union bound
[
24
, Fact.3]
, (c) follows from
\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}\right)\left(\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}=0
, since
\rho^{Y_{1}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}
lives in
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
and
I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}}
is a projector onto the complement of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}
, (d) follows from the trace inequality
\tr(\Delta\rho)\leq\tr(\Delta\sigma)+\frac{1}{2}\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
for
0\leq\Delta,\rho,\sigma\leq I
, (e) follows from
(i) Proposition
5
and from (ii)
[
24
, Corollary 1]
, (f) follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}^{\mathcal{J}}_{v_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
for
\mathcal{J}\in\{0,1,2\}
in (
10
) and from (ii) Gelfand–Naimark’s Theorem
[
43
, Thm. 3.7]
, and finally (g) follows from the property established at the beginning of this appendix, for
\mathcal{J}\in\{0,1,2\}
. Setting
\eta=\epsilon^{1/5}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.1}]\leq 16\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}+72\epsilon^{\frac{3}{5}}+144\epsilon^{\frac{1}{10}}.

Appendix E
Proof of Proposition
7

For simplicity, we provide analysis only for terms
T_{1.2.2},T_{1.2.3}
and
T_{1.2.4}
. The other terms can be analyzed analogously.

Analysis of
T_{1.2.2}
:
We evaluate the expectation of
T_{1.2.2}
over
\left(D_{1}^{n}(b_{1},m_{1}),D_{2}^{n}(a),V_{1}^{n}(\tilde{b}_{1},\tilde{m}_{1}),D_{1}^{n}(\tilde{b}_{1},\tilde{m}_{1})\right)
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{2n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

(52)

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}\neq m_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

where (a) follows by using the definition
\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}|U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n}|u^{n})\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}
and (b) follows by using

\displaystyle\theta_{u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}=\frac{1+\eta^{2}}{1+2\eta^{2}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
24
]
. Consider the first term in (
52
). We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\>\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\pi_{\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}\right)}\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}}\right)}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\overline{B}^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right)\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{G^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\>\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\pi^{Y_{1}}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(h)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta)}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta-(R_{1}+K_{1}))}2^{-n(I(V_{1};Y_{1}|U)-2\delta)},

where (a) follows from

\displaystyle\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\leq\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right)\leq\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right),

(b) follows from inserting the projector

\pi_{\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{G}})}
, (c) follows because the support of
I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}}}-\beta^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}^{n}_{1},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}
is contained in
\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}^{e}\right)
, (d) follows because
\mathcal{T}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}
is an isometry, (e) follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
in (
10
) and from (ii) the Gelfand–Naimark’s Theorem
[
43
, Thm. 3.7]
, (f) follows from the definition of
G^{1}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}
in (
11
),
(g) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (h) follows from

\displaystyle\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\leq\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta)}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}),

(53)

and finally (i) follows from the typical projector property

\displaystyle\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\pi_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y_{1}|U)-\delta\right)},\mbox{ and }\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{1}}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n}}\right\}\leq 2^{n\left(H(Y_{1}|V_{1},U)+\delta\right)},\mbox{ if }(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in\mathcal{T}_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U}).

Now, consider the second term in (
52
). We have,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n}}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}p_{V_{1}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n})p_{U}^{n}(u^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{d_{2}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{u^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{a}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})}2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta)}p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})

\displaystyle\norm{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_1^n,\tilde{d}_1^n,u^n,d_2^n}}_{1}\norm{\CalN_{d_2^n,\eta}\left( \rho_{u^n}^{Y_1} \otimes\ketbra{0} \right)}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta-(R_{1}+K_{1}))}2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}\frac{3\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}}

where (a) follows because
\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}=0
, if
(\tilde{v}_{1}^{n},u^{n})\notin T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{1}U})
, (b) follows from (i) the inequality in (
53
) and from (ii) the trace inequality

\displaystyle|\tr\left(AB\right)|\leq\norm{AB}_{1}\leq\min\{\norm{A}_{1}\norm{B}_{\infty},\norm{A}_{\infty}\norm{B}_{1}\},

(54)

and (c) follows from (i) the inequality

\displaystyle\norm{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{v}_1^n,\tilde{d}^n_1,u^n,d_2^n}}_{1}\leq\norm{I_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_1}^{e}}}- \beta^{*}_{\tilde{v}_1^n,\tilde{d}^n_1,u^n,d_2^n}}_{\infty}^{2}\norm{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_1}^G}}}_{1}\leq 2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n},

(55)

and from (ii) the inequality

\displaystyle\norm{\CalN_{d_2^n,\eta}\left( \rho_{u^n}^{Y_1} \otimes\ketbra{0} \right)}_{\infty}\leq\frac{3\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}}

as stated in
[
24
]
. Therefore, if we choose
|\mathcal{D}_{1}|\geq\left(3\eta 2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}2^{n\left(I(V_{1};Y_{1}|U)-2\delta\right)}\right)^{\frac{2}{n}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[T_{1.2.2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n(I(V_{1};U)-3\delta-(R_{1}+K_{1}))}\left(2^{-n\left(I(V_{1};Y_{1}|U)-2\delta\right)}+\frac{2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}3\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\>2^{-n\left(I(V_{1};Y_{1},U)-5\delta-(R_{1}+K_{1})\right)}.

Therefore, for sufficiently large
n
, we have
\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.2}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle R_{1}+K_{1}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(V_{1};Y_{1},U)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H(V_{1})-H(V_{1}|Y_{1},U).

Analysis of
T_{1.2.3}
:

We evaluate the expectation of
T_{1.2.3}
with respect to
\left(D_{1}^{n}(b_{1},m_{1}),D_{2}^{n}(a),U^{n}(\tilde{a}),D_{2}^{n}(\tilde{a})\right)
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.3}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{\upsilon^{s_{\mathsf{b}}+t_{\mathsf{b}}}}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{2n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{a}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{a}\neq a}\sum_{\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}^{n}}q_{U}^{n}(\tilde{u}^{n})p_{V_{1}U}^{n}(v_{1}^{n},u^{n})

(56)

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},\tilde{u}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{a}\neq a}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}^{n}}q_{U}^{n}(\tilde{u}^{n})p_{V_{1}}^{n}(v_{1}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},\tilde{u}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{a}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}^{n}}q_{U}^{n}(\tilde{u}^{n})p_{V_{1}}^{n}(v_{1}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},\tilde{u}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\>\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{v_{1}^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{M}_{1}|}\frac{1}{2^{nK_{1}}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{m_{1}}\sum_{b_{1}}\sum_{\tilde{a}}\sum_{d_{1}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{1}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}^{n}}q_{U}^{n}(\tilde{u}^{n})p_{V_{1}}^{n}(v_{1}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},\tilde{u}^{n},\tilde{d}_{2}^{n}}\>\mathcal{N}_{d_{1}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{v_{1}^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

where (a) follows by using the definition
\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}}\sum_{d_{2}^{n}}\sum_{u^{n}}p_{U|V_{1}}^{n}(u^{n}|v_{1}^{n})\>\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n},u^{n},d_{2}^{n}}
, and (b) follows by using

\displaystyle\theta_{v_{1}^{n},d_{1}^{n}}=\frac{1+\eta^{2}}{1+2\eta^{2}}\mathcal{T}_{d_{1}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{v_{1}^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{d_{1}^{n},\eta}\left(\rho_{v_{1}^{n}}^{Y_{1}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
24
]
. We begin by bounding the first term in (
56
). We have

]
. Therefore, if we choose

\max\{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|,|\mathcal{D}_{2}|\}\geq\left(3\eta 2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}2^{n\left(D(p_{U}||q_{U})+I(V_{1};U)+I(V_{1},U;Y_{1})-\tilde{\delta}-5\delta\right)}\right)^{\frac{2}{n}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.4}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U}||q_{U})+I(V_{1};U)-3\delta-\tilde{\delta}-(R_{1}+K_{1}+\max\{S_{2}+T_{2},S_{3}+T_{3}\})\right)}

\displaystyle\left(2^{-n\left(I(V_{1},U;Y_{1})-2\delta\right)}+2^{n}|\mathcal{H}_{Y_{1}}|^{n}\frac{3\eta}{\sqrt{\max\{|\mathcal{D}_{1}|^{n},|\mathcal{D}_{2}|^{n}\}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\>2^{-n\left(D(p_{U}||q_{U})+I(V_{1};U)+I(V_{1},U;Y_{1})-\tilde{\delta}-5\delta-(R_{1}+K_{1}+\max\{S_{2}+T_{2},S_{3}+T_{3}\})\right)}

Hence, for sufficiently large
n
, we have
\mathbb{E}[T_{1.2.4}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle R_{1}+K_{1}+\max\{S_{2}+T_{2},S_{3}+T_{3}\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle D(p_{U}||q_{U})+I(V_{1};U)+I(V_{1},U;Y_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log(\Prime)+H(V_{1})-H(V_{1},U|Y_{1}).

Appendix F
Proof of Proposition
9

Observe that

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\right\}-\norm{\pi^{Y_2}_{v_2^n} \: \rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2} \: \pi^{Y_2}_{v_2^n}-\rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2}}_{1}-\norm{\pi^{Y_2} \: \rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2} \: \pi^{Y_2}-\rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2}}_{1}

\displaystyle-\norm{\pi^{Y_2}_{u_2^n} \: \rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2} \: \pi^{Y_2}_{u_2^n}-\rho_{u_2^n, v_2^n}^{Y_2}}_{1}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\geq}

\displaystyle 1-\epsilon-6\sqrt{\epsilon},

where (a) follows from the definition of

\Upsilon_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}
in (
14
), (b) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (c) follows from applying the inequality
\tr(\Delta\rho)\geq\tr(\Delta\sigma)-\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, for
0\leq\Delta,\rho,\sigma\leq I
, three times, and (d) follows from (i)

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,

and from (ii) the Gentle Operator Lemma
[
25
]
, since each typical projector satisfies

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\>\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon,\mbox{ and }\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

Therefore, we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1-(1-\epsilon-6\sqrt{\epsilon})=\epsilon+6\sqrt{\epsilon}.

Appendix G
Proof of Proposition
10

Analysis of
T_{2.2.2}
:

We evaluate the expectation of
T_{2.2.2}
over the random choice of
V^{n}_{2}(\tilde{b}_{2},\tilde{m}_{22})
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}\neq m_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}\neq m_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}|U_{2}})}p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}|U_{2}})}2^{-n(I(V_{2};U_{2})-3\delta)}2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}

\displaystyle p_{V_{2}|U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}|u_{2}^{n})p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}2^{-n(I(V_{2};U_{2})-3\delta)}2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}2^{-n(I(V_{2};U_{2})-3\delta)}2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2})-\delta\right)}

\displaystyle p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(I(V_{2};Y_{2},U_{2})-5\delta\right)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(I(V_{2};Y_{2},U_{2})-5\delta\right)}p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\rho_{v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{=}

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(I(V_{2};Y_{2},U_{2})-5\delta-(L_{2}+K_{2})\right)}

where (a) follows from the definition of
\Upsilon_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}
in (
14
), (b) follows from (i) the inequality

\displaystyle p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\leq 2^{-n(I(V_{2};U_{2})-3\delta)}p_{V_{2}|U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}|u_{2}^{n}),\mbox{ for }\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}|U_{2}})

and from (ii) the fact that if
(u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})
, then

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}},

(c) follows from
\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}=\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}p_{V_{2}|U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}|u_{2}^{n})\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}
, (d) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2})-\delta\right)}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}},\mbox{ for }u_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}})

(e) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\leq\pi^{Y_{2}}\leq I,

(f) follows from
\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}=\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}|V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}|v_{2}^{n})\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}
, and finally (g) follows from the cyclicity of the trace and from
\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\leq I
.
Therefore, for
n
sufficiently large, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.2}]\leq\epsilon,\mbox{ if }L_{2}+K_{2}<I(V_{2};Y_{2},U_{2}).

Analysis of
T_{2.2.3}
:

We evaluate the expectation of
T_{2.2.3}
with respect to the random choice of
U^{n}_{2}(\tilde{a}_{2},\tilde{m}_{21})
. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.3}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}\neq m_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}\neq m_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}})}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})+\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}\!\!\!2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}2^{-n\left(I(U_{2};Y_{2}|V_{2})-2\delta\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\overset{(h)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}\!\!\!2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}2^{-n(I(U_{2};V_{2})-3\delta)}

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(I(U_{2};Y_{2}|V_{2})-2\delta\right)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})+I(U_{2};Y_{2},V_{2})-2\delta-\delta_{2}-(S_{2}+T_{2})\right)},

where (a) follows from
\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}=\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}p^{n}_{U_{2}|V_{2}}(u_{2}^{n}|v_{2}^{n})\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}
, (b) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2}|V_{2})-\delta\right)}\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}},\mbox{ if }v_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{V_{2}}),

(c) follows from the definition of
\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}
in (
14
), (d) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (e) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\leq\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\leq\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\leq I,

(f) follows from

\displaystyle\tr\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}\}\leq 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})+\delta\right)},\mbox{ for }(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}}),

(g) follows from

\displaystyle q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})\leq 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}),\mbox{ if }\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}}),

(61)

and finally (h) follows from

\displaystyle\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\leq 2^{-n(I(U_{2};V_{2})-3\delta)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}).

Therefore, for
n
sufficiently large, we have
\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.3}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle S_{2}+T_{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})+I(U_{2};Y_{2},V_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log(\Prime)-H(U_{2}|Y_{2},V_{2}).

Analysis of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}\neq m_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}\neq m_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}\neq m_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}\neq m_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{v_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\rho_{v_{2}^{n}}^{Y_{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\sum_{\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2})-\delta\right)}q_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(h)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}2^{-n\left(I(U_{2},V_{2};Y_{2})-2\delta\right)}

\displaystyle p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{n(R_{2}+S_{2}+K_{2})}}\sum_{m_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\sum_{b_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{22}}\sum_{\tilde{b}_{2}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{21}}\sum_{\tilde{a}_{2}}\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})-\delta_{2}\right)}2^{-n(I(U_{2};V_{2})-3\delta)}

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(I(U_{2},V_{2};Y_{2})-2\delta\right)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-n\left(D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})+I(U_{2};V_{2})+I(U_{2},V_{2};Y_{2})-5\delta-\delta_{2}-(L_{2}+K_{2}+S_{2}+T_{2})\right)}

where (a) follows from
\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}=\sum_{u_{2}^{n}}p_{U_{2}|V_{2}}^{n}(u_{2}^{n}|v_{2}^{n})\rho^{Y_{2}}_{u_{2}^{n},v_{2}^{n}}
, (b) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}=\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}=\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}\>\sqrt{\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}}\leq\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}},

(c) follows from
\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}=\sum_{v_{2}^{n}}p_{V_{2}}^{n}(v_{2}^{n})\rho^{Y_{2}}_{v_{2}^{n}}
, (d) follows from the definition of
\Upsilon_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}
in (
14
), (e) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, (f) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}\left(\rho^{Y_{2}}\right)^{\otimes n}\pi^{Y_{2}}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y_{2})-\delta\right)}\pi^{Y_{2}},

(g) follows from

\displaystyle\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}\>\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\leq\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n}}\leq I,

(h) follows from (
61
) and from

\displaystyle\tr\{\pi^{Y_{2}}_{\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}}\}\leq 2^{n\left(H(Y_{2}|U_{2},V_{2})-\delta\right)},\mbox{ for }(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}}),

and finally (i) follows from

\displaystyle\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}p_{U_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n})p_{V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\leq\sum_{(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{U_{2}V_{2}})}2^{-n(I(U_{2};V_{2})-3\delta)}p_{U_{2}V_{2}}^{n}(\tilde{u}_{2}^{n},\tilde{v}_{2}^{n}),

Therefore, for
n
sufficiently large, we have
\mathbb{E}[T_{2.2.4}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle L_{2}+K_{2}+S_{2}+T_{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle D(p_{U_{2}}||q_{U_{2}})+I(U_{2};V_{2})+I(U_{2},V_{2};Y_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log(\Prime)+H(V_{2})-H(U_{2};V_{2}|Y_{2}).

Appendix H
Proof of Proposition
12

We begin by establishing a key property of the decoding POVM elements
G_{\underline{z}^{n}}^{S}
for
S\subseteq[4]
, which will be used throughout this appendix.

\displaystyle\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left(G^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right)\geq 1-\epsilon-2\sqrt{\epsilon}.

The proof follows the same arguments as in Appendix
D
. Now, we evaluate the expectation of
T_{1.1}
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\gamma^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(62)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\left[\tr\left\{\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}-\tr\left\{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y}^{e}}}-\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}\right\}\right],

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}+\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}+\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\norm{\theta_{\ulinez^n, \ulined^n}-\left(\xi_{\ulinez^n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{1},

where (a) follows from (i) the definition of
\gamma^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}
in (
38
), (ii) the cyclicity of the trace and (iii) the property of the projector i.e.,
\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}^{2}=\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}
, (b) follows from the non-commutative union bound
[
24
, Fact.3]
and (c) follows from the trace inequality
\tr(\Lambda\rho)\leq\tr(\Lambda\sigma)+\frac{1}{2}\norm{\rho- \sigma}_{1}
, with
0\leq\Lambda,\rho,\sigma\leq I
.
Using Proposition
11
, we bound the second term by
12\eta
and since the state
\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}
lives in
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}
and
I-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}
is a projector onto its complement, we are left with

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\beta^{*}_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}+12\eta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{42}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\left[\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}\tr\left\{\overline{B}^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\left(\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}\right]+12\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{42}{\eta^{2}}\frac{4}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\left[15-\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p^{n}_{\underline{Z}}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{S}_{\underline{z}^{n}}\xi_{\underline{z}^{n}}\right\}\right]+12\eta.

where the second inequality follows from
[
24
, Corollary.1]
and the equality follows from (i) the definition of
\overline{B}_{\underline{z}^{n}}^{S}
in (
36
) and (ii) from Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
43
, Thm. 3.7]
. Hence, by applying the property established at the beginning of this appendix, for
S\subseteq[4]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.1}]\leq\frac{168}{\eta^{2}}(15\epsilon+30\sqrt{\epsilon})+12\eta.

With
\eta=\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.1}]\leq 168(15\epsilon^{\frac{3}{5}}+30\epsilon^{\frac{1}{10}})+12\epsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}

Appendix I
Proof of Proposition
13

We elaborate on the analysis of
\sum_{S\subseteq[4]}T_{1S}
, and in doing so we leverage the
smoothing
and
augmentation

[
24
]
properties of the tilting maps we have defined in (
34
). As is standard, this is broken into fifteen terms—four singleton errors, six pair errors, four triple errors, and one quadruple error. The analysis of the quadruple error is slightly different from the analysis of the remaining fourteen terms. To analyze the quadruple error, we first prove that the difference between the average of the original state and the average of the tilted state over
\mathcal{\underline{Z}}^{n}
is small. This is done analogously to the steps used in
[
24
]
. Having done this, the rest of the analysis is straightforward, since the corresponding projector has not been tilted, and we are left with it operating on the average of the original state.

We evaluate the expectation of
T_{1.S}
for
S=4
over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.4}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{2n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}\neq\underline{m}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(63)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}\neq\underline{m}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\theta^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\},

where (a) follows by using the definition
\theta^{\otimes n}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}
,
and (b) follows by using

\displaystyle\theta^{\otimes n}=\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right),

as stated in
[
24
]
. Consider the first term in (
63
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega(\eta)}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\overline{B}^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\right)\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\xi^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi\pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\pi\xi^{\otimes n}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\pi\xi^{\otimes n}\pi\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}2^{-n\left(H(Y)-\delta\right)}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}2^{-n(I(Y;\underline{Z})-2\delta)},

where (a) follows from
\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\leq I-\beta^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\leq I-\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}
, (b) follows from
\beta^{[4]}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\overline{B}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}^{[4]}
, (c) follows from (i)
\overline{B}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}^{[4]}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}I-\overline{G}^{[4]}_{\tilde{\underline{z}}^{n}}
and (ii) from the Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
43
, Thm. 3.7]
, (d) follows from the definition of
G^{[4]}_{\tilde{\underline{z}}^{n}}

in (
35
), (e) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, and finally (f) and (g) follows from the standard typical projector property

\displaystyle\pi\xi^{\otimes n}\pi\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y)-\delta\right)},\mbox{ and }\tr\{\pi_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}\}\leq 2^{n\left(H(Y|\underline{Z})+\delta\right)},\mbox{ for }\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}}).

Next, consider the second term in (
63
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n},\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\mathcal{N}_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{\underline{m}}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{d}}^{n}}\sum_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\uline{\tilde{z}}^{n})\norm{ \gamma^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{1}\norm{ \CalN_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right) }_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}\>2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}},

The first inequality follows from the standard trace-norm inequality

\displaystyle|\tr(AB)|\leq\norm{AB}_{1}\leq\norm{A}_{1}\norm{B}_{\infty}.

(64)

The last inequality follows from (i)

\displaystyle\norm{\gamma^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{1}\leq\norm{I-\beta^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{\infty}\norm{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y}^{e}}}- \beta^{*}_{\ulinez^{n},\ulined^{n}}\right)}_{1}\leq\norm{I-\beta^*_{\uline{\tilde{z}}^n, \uline{\tilde{d}}^n}}_{\infty}^{2}\norm{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }}_{1}\leq 2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n},

and (ii) from the inequality stated in
[
24
]

\displaystyle\norm{ \CalN_{\eta}\left(\xi^{\otimes n} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right) }_{\infty}\leq\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}},

which implies that the difference between
\theta^{\otimes n}
and
\left(\xi^{\otimes n}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)
is small. This establishes the smoothing property. Therefore, if we choose
|\underline{D}|\geq(42\eta^{2})^{\frac{1}{n}}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{2}2^{2(I(Y;\underline{Z})+2\delta)}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.4}]\leq 2^{n(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})}\left(2^{-n\left(I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right)-2\delta\right)}+\frac{42|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}}\right)\leq 2\>2^{-n\left(I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right)-2\delta-(\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4})\right)}.

Hence, if

\displaystyle\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}+\tilde{R}_{3}+\tilde{R}_{4}<I\left(Y;\underline{Z}\right),\mbox{ then }\mathbb{E}[T_{1.4}]\leq\epsilon.

Returning to the original notation (see Fig.
8
), we have

\mathbb{E}[T_{1.4}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle S_{ji}+T_{ji}+S_{jk}+T_{jk}+K_{j}+L_{j}+\max\{S_{ij}+T_{ij},S_{kj}+T_{kj}\}<I(Y_{j};U_{ji},U_{jk},V_{j},U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj}).

The above bound is obtained when binning is ignored. However, since our analysis includes binning and, moreover, each of the codebooks is picked according to the corresponding marginal distribution, the right-hand side of the bound we obtain will contain an additional divergence term. Specifically, we obtain

\displaystyle S_{ji}+T_{ji}+S_{jk}+T_{jk}+K_{j}+L_{j}+\max\{S_{ij}+T_{ij},S_{kj}+T_{kj}\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(Y_{j};U_{ji},U_{jk},V_{j},U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj})+D\left(p_{U_{ji}U_{jk}V_{j}U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj}}\Big|\Big|\frac{p_{V_{j}}}{|\mathcal{U}_{ji}||\mathcal{U}_{jk}|\upsilon_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{ji}|\right)+\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{jk}|\right)+\log\left(\upsilon_{j}\right)+H(V_{j})-H(U_{ji},U_{jk},V_{j},U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj}|Y_{j}).

Note that the above bound is identical to the one obtained in (
31
), (
32
)
of the Thm.
3
, with
\mathcal{A}_{j}=\{ji,jk\}
.

Having described the analysis of the quadruple error term, we are left with fourteen terms. The analysis of each of these fourteen terms is essentially identical. Therefore, we describe the analysis for a general
S\subsetneq[4]
.
Consider

\displaystyle T_{1.S}=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{Z^{n}_{S}(\tilde{m}_{S}),D^{n}_{S}(\tilde{m}_{S}),Z^{n}_{S^{c}}(m_{S^{c}}),D^{n}_{S^{c}}(m_{S^{c}})}\theta_{\underline{Z}^{n}(\underline{m}),\underline{D}^{n}(\underline{m})}\right\}.

We evaluate the expectation over the codebook generation distribution. We obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.S}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\underline{d}^{n}}\sum_{\underline{z}^{n}}p_{\underline{Z}}^{n}(\underline{z}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}\right\}

(65)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}\neq m_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\theta_{z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\},

where (a) follows by using the definition
\theta_{z^{n}_{S},d^{n}_{S}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}_{S^{c}}|^{n}}\sum_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\sum_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}|Z_{S}}^{n}(z^{n}_{S^{c}}|z^{n}_{S})\theta_{\underline{z}^{n},\underline{d}^{n}}
, and (b) follows by using

\displaystyle\theta{z^{n}_{S},d^{n}_{S}}=\frac{\Omega(S,\eta)}{\Omega(\eta)}\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)+\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right).

Consider the first term in (
65
). We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\pi_{\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})}\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}^{S^{c}}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})}-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}-\overline{B}^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right)\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{G^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z_{S^{c}}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(f)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z_{S^{c}}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}})}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\>\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(g)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)}2^{-n(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta)},

where (a) follows from
\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\leq I-\beta^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\leq I-\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}
,
(b) follows from
\beta^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}
having support contained in
\mathcal{T}^{S^{c}}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}(\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}})
, (c) follows from extracting the isometry
\mathcal{T}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}},\eta}
, (d) follows from (i)
\overline{B}^{S}_{\tilde{z}_{S}^{n},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathrel{\hbox{\hskip 3.8889pt\hskip-3.8889pt\hbox{$=$}\hskip-3.8889pt\hskip-3.28473pt\raisebox{4.66875pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Delta$}}\hskip-3.28473pt\hskip 3.8889pt}}I_{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}_{Y_{G}}}}-\overline{G}^{S}_{\tilde{z}_{S}^{n},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}
and (ii) from Gelfand–Naimark’s Thm.
[
43
, Thm. 3.7]
, (e) follows from the definition of
G^{S}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}
in (
35
), (f) follows from the cyclicity of the trace, and finally (g) follows from

\displaystyle\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\pi_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y|Z_{S^{c}})-\delta\right)},\mbox{ and }\tr\{\pi_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\}\leq 2^{-n\left(H(Y|\underline{Z})+\delta\right)},\mbox{ if }(\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},z_{S^{c}}^{n})\in T_{\delta}^{n}(p_{\underline{Z}}).

Now consider the second term in (
65
).

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\tr\left\{\gamma^{*}_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S},\tilde{d}^{n}_{S},z^{n}_{S^{c}},d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\mathcal{N}_{d^{n}_{S^{c}}}\left(\xi_{z^{n}_{S^{c}}}\otimes\outerproduct{0}{0}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{M}}|}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{\underline{D}}|^{n}}\sum_{\underline{m}}\sum_{\tilde{m}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{d}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{\tilde{z}^{n}_{S}}\sum_{d_{S^{c}}^{n}}\sum_{z_{S^{c}}^{n}}p_{Z_{S^{c}}}^{n}(z_{S^{c}}^{n})p_{Z_{S}}^{n}(\tilde{z}_{S}^{n})\norm{ \gamma^*_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}}_{1}\norm{\CalN_{d^n_{S^c}}\left(\xi_{z^n_{S^c}} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{\infty}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)}\>2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\>\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}}.

where (a) follows from the trace-norm inequality given in (
64
) and (b) follows from (i)

\displaystyle\norm{\gamma^*_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\norm{I-\beta^*_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}}_{\infty}\norm{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }\left(I_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y}^{e}}}- \beta^{*}_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}\right)}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\norm{I-\beta^*_{\tilde{z}^n_S, \tilde{d}^n_S,z^n_{S^c}, d^n_{S^c}}}_{\infty}^{2}\norm{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\CalH_{Y_G}} }}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n},

and from (ii) the inequality

\displaystyle\norm{\mathcal{N}_{d^n_S, \eta}\left( \xi_{z^n_S} \otimes\ketbra{0}\right)}_{\infty}\leq\frac{21\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D_{S}}|^{n}}},

as stated in
[
24
]
. Therefore, if we choose
|\mathcal{D}_{S}|\geq(42\eta)^{\frac{2}{n}}|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{2}2^{2\left(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta\right)}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{1.S}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{n\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)}\left(2^{-n(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta)}+\frac{42|\mathcal{H}_{Y}|^{n}\eta}{\sqrt{|\mathcal{D}_{S}|^{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 22^{-n\left(I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}})-2\delta-\left(\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}\right)\right)}

Hence,
\mathbb{E}[T_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\sum_{s\in S}\tilde{R}_{s}<I(Y;Z_{S}|Z_{S^{c}}).

As an example, we take
S=\{1,2\}
, i.e.,
\mathbb{E}[T_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle\tilde{R}_{1}+\tilde{R}_{2}<I(Y;Z_{1},Z_{2}|Z_{3},Z_{4}).

Returning to the original notation (see Fig.
8
), we have
\mathbb{E}[T_{1.S}]\leq\epsilon,
if

\displaystyle S_{ji}+T_{ji}+S_{jk}+T_{jk}<I(Y_{j};U_{ji},U_{jk}|V_{j},U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj}).

Now if the analysis is carried out with binning, we obtain the following bound

\displaystyle S_{ji}+T_{ji}+S_{jk}+T_{jk}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle I(Y_{j};U_{ji},U_{jk}|V_{j},U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj})+D\left(p_{U_{ji}U_{jk}}\Big|\Big|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{ji}||\mathcal{U}_{jk}|}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{ji}|\right)+\log\left(|\mathcal{U}_{jk}|\right)-H\left(U_{ji},U_{jk}|U_{ij}\oplus U_{kj},V_{j},Y_{j}\right),

which corresponds to the bound (
27
) in Thm.
3
, with
\mathcal{A}_{j}=\{ji,jk\}
.
